# Change Log

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.
See [Conventional Commits](https://conventionalcommits.org) for commit guidelines.

# [4.1.0](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/compare/v4.0.0...v4.1.0) (2026-10-13)

### Bug Fixes

* a family too thin to meet its moved self gates its structure at none, the morph test's selection never fills, and the terrain notes halve the blend span ([0a33480](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/0a33480b03b7b1349f2784e3cbeaaf0cdbdf207d))
* **build:** the dev server's package build runs no shell, since WSL's bash can come first on Windows ([2021e03](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/2021e034cb23b6377cb039b77da54e1ab16ae860))
* CI's lint, format and stale tests on ai/queue ([00520ac](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/00520ac43384d6d2fe66858df17cbe7030d993f4))
* CI's oxlint errors on ai/queue ([f9871f1](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/f9871f14256b9a6d37573dcdc9ed5752b1ede448))
* **ci:** the package builds carry the sources a build hook writes under src/generated, so a typecheck job finds the generated kit map beside the barrels ([7cb21f7](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/7cb21f73922aabf15267522a3f710f5ec8b6c6a7))
* clusterColours updates its means in place, and the genshin types size snapshots follow their builds ([35ca970](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/35ca970bedfbed3ea4d37a89aff59df6adeca2d0))

### Features

* **coderabbit:** a window has no minimum size, so the queue drains instead of waiting ([997dd50](https://github.com/Esposter/Esposter/commit/997dd50d2b1a45eb732ba065d664249e45551385))

### Performance Improvements
